# Statistics kit v2: the old kit against the new kit on every published comparison

**No published number changes.** Every comparison output committed under `docs/benchmarks/` was rerun twice from the same checkout of main (`4f95d6dce`), once with the kit as it is on main (kit v1, the kit every one of these runs was preregistered with) and once with kit v2 copied over `runner/stats/`. Across 37 output files from 18 runs there are 1245 intervals, 949 of them with a p-value. None of them change: every p-value, Holm-adjusted p and interval is the same to the last digit.

- **Why nothing moves.** The published comparisons use `clusteredPairedDelta` (interval and two-sided sign-flip p), `holmAdjusted` and `exactMcNemar`. Kit v2 leaves those values alone. It changes `nonInferiorityP` and `superiorityP` (no p when nothing varies, and superiority as the exact sign-flip p), `rows.ts` and `gates.ts`. No published comparison used `superiorityP`, `rows.ts` or a `gates.ts` family, and none used `compare-run --margin`, the one path to `nonInferiorityP`.
- **Where outputs differ at all.** In the eight install-ladder files only `exposure.grader_dirty` and `exposure.problems` differ, because the second pass ran with `runner/stats/` modified in the scratch worktree. Those fields record the checkout, not a statistic.
- **Not rerun.** `summarize-run.ts`, `between-run.ts`, `scripts/v03/pilot-tables.ts`, `scripts/tool-discipline/`, `scripts/paired-mdd.ts` and `scripts/prompt-archaeology/tables.ts` take only `seededRandom`, `normalQuantile`, `minimumDiscordantForSignificance`, `holmAdjusted` or `clusteredPairedDelta`'s interval and two-sided p from the kit, and those functions are unchanged in v2. `scripts/sealed/export.ts` has no published opening under `docs/benchmarks/` yet.
- **What keeps it that way.** These runs are analysed with the kit at their preregistration commit anyway (`runner/stats/kit.ts`), so this table checks what a dated amendment moving one of them to kit v2 would change today: nothing.

## How it was rerun

1. `git worktree add --detach <scratch> origin/main` at `4f95d6dce`, so no file under a counted run's directory in the working checkout was written.
2. Each command below, run in the worktree with a scrubbed environment. The command is rebuilt from the output's file name and recorded settings (`--against`, `--pair gbrain`, `--design`, `--families`; seed 7 and 10,000 draws are the defaults every file records).
3. **Reproduction check.** The first pass was compared with the committed files leaf by leaf, and every p-value, interval and estimate in a committed file came back identical. Thirteen files differ only outside those numbers: eleven gain fields that later versions of the scripts write (the `faults_as_failures` sensitivity row, the install ladder's `exposure` block, and in `v02-heldout-2026-10`'s secondary endpoints one more endpoint, so that file was matched by endpoint name), and the two `installer-cc-codex-2026-11` files record this checkout's grader commit and its exposure problems in place of the original ones.
4. `runner/stats/{paired,rows,gates,sign-flip,task-means,sizing}.ts` from this branch copied into the worktree, the same commands run again, and the two passes compared leaf by leaf.

## Results, one table per output file

Columns: the comparison (the identifying fields of its JSON entry), and for each kit the p-values the entry carries (`p` two-sided sign-flip, `holm_p` Holm-adjusted within its family) and its 95% task-clustered interval. An entry with no p-value is a descriptive interval.
